  1153	# ── Daemon ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1154	
  1155	
  1156	class PipelineCoordinator:
  1157	    """Skeleton coordinator daemon: tick → evaluate → log no-op → heartbeat.
  1158	
  1159	    Collaborators are injected for deterministic tests; ``from_env`` /
  1160	    default construction wires the production defaults. The daemon holds
  1161	    no durable state of its own beyond an in-memory work-graph cache STUB
  1162	    that is rebuilt every tick (ADR-0021 §3.2 stateless-across-restarts).
  1163	    """
  1164	
  1165	    def __init__(
  1340	            now=self._clock(),
  1341	            capacity=self._capacity_provider(),
  1342	            mode=self._mode_selector.select(),
  1343	            situation=situation,
  1344	            mode_override=mode_override,
  1345	            ticket=wg.focal,
  1346	            tickets=dict(wg.tickets),
  1347	        )
  1348	
  1349	    # ── decision-log record (ADR-0021 §3.2 example) ──
  1350	
  1351	    def _build_tick_record(
  1352	        self,
  1353	        result: DecisionResult,
  1354	        action_results: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
  1355	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1356	        # A no-op tick records ``actions:[]``; only real (non-noop) actions
  1357	        # are serialised into the array (skeleton emits none).
  1358	        actions = [a.to_record() for a in result.actions if not isinstance(a, NoopAction)]
  1359	        dry_run = all(a.dry_run for a in result.actions) if result.actions else True
  1360	        record: dict[str, Any] = {
  1361	            "ts": self._clock().isoformat(),
  1362	            "event": "decision_tick",
  1363	            "engine_version": result.engine_version,
  1364	            "mode": result.mode,
  1365	            "decision_id": result.decision_id,
  1366	            "actions": actions,
  1367	            "reason": result.reason,
  1368	            "dry_run": dry_run,
  1369	            "pid": os.getpid(),
  1370	        }
  1371	        # When a personality mode fired (non-idle tick), record the behavior
  1372	        # knobs that steered Tier-1 / Tier-2 so the selected mode is auditable
  1373	        # in the shadow/decision log (ADR-0021 §7.2 + Integration AC).
  1374	        if result.mode_behavior is not None:
  1375	            record["mode_behavior"] = {
  1376	                "tier1_dominant": result.mode_behavior.tier1_dominant,
  1377	                "tier2_policy": result.mode_behavior.tier2_policy,
  1378	                "llm_context_hops": result.mode_behavior.llm_context_hops,
  1379	                "llm_lessons_window": result.mode_behavior.llm_lessons_window,
  1380	            }
  1381	        # 29f-3: name the firing rule + tier (ADR Appendix C) and the action
  1382	        # layer's per-action outcomes — only when a rule actually fired, so a
  1383	        # plain no-op tick keeps the skeleton's minimal record shape.
  1384	        if result.rule_name is not None:
  1385	            record["rule_name"] = result.rule_name
  1386	        if result.tier is not None:
  1387	            record["tier"] = result.tier
  1388	        # 29f-6: the Tier-2 LLM-consultation block (ADR Appendix C) — only on a
  1389	        # tick that actually consulted (or degraded) the LLM, so a Tier-1 / no-op
  1390	        # tick keeps its minimal record shape and ``budget_cap`` events stay
  1391	        # grep-able + replayable for the daily-spend rebuild.
  1392	        if result.llm_consultation is not None:
  1393	            record["llm_consultation"] = dict(result.llm_consultation)
  1394	        if action_results:
  1395	            record["action_results"] = action_results
  1396	        return record
  1397	
  1398	    # ── cold-start 4-phase recovery (ADR §3.3 + §9 L6) ──
  1399	
  1400	    def _log_cold_start(self, record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
  1401	        """Append a cold-start decision-log record, stamping ts/pid/engine."""
  1402	        base: dict[str, Any] = {
  1403	            "ts": self._clock().isoformat(),
  1404	            "engine_version": self._engine.engine_version,
  1405	            "pid": os.getpid(),
  1406	        }
  1407	        base.update(record)
  1408	        self._decision_log.append(base)
  1409	
  1410	    def startup(self) -> ColdStartReport:
  1411	        """Run the 4-phase cold-start recovery (ADR §3.3). Returns a report.
  1412	
  1413	        Order: Startup-1 (infra) → L6 crash-recovery (ADR §9 L6: "after Phase
  1414	        Startup-1") → Startup-2 (JIRA reconcile) → Startup-3 (stale sweep) →
  1415	        Startup-4 (log steady-state entry). If Startup-1 cannot bring the
  1490	        last_real_action_tick: dict[str, Any] | None = None
  1491	        for rec in records:
  1492	            event = rec.get("event")
  1493	            if event == "decision_tick" and not rec.get("dry_run", True) and rec.get("actions"):
  1494	                last_real_action_tick = rec
  1495	            elif event == "shutdown_began":
  1496	                began[str(rec.get("decision_id", ""))] = rec
  1497	                # Snapshot which real-action tick preceded this drain.
  1498	                rec["_preceding_real_tick"] = last_real_action_tick
  1499	            elif event == "shutdown_complete":
  1500	                completed.add(str(rec.get("decision_id", "")))
  1501	        for drain_id, drain_rec in began.items():
  1502	            if drain_id in completed:
  1503	                continue  # clean shutdown — nothing to recover
  1504	            tick = drain_rec.get("_preceding_real_tick")
  1505	            resolutions = self._resolve_interrupted_actions(tick)
  1506	            entry = {
  1507	                "event": CRASH_RECOVERY_EVENT,
  1508	                "crashed_drain_id": drain_id,
  1509	                "resolutions": resolutions,
  1510	            }
  1511	            report.crash_recoveries.append(entry)
  1512	            self._log_cold_start(entry)
  1513	
  1514	    def _resolve_interrupted_actions(self, tick: dict[str, Any] | None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  1515	        """Map each interrupted action of a crashed tick to a recovery step."""
  1516	        if not tick:
  1517	            return [{"resolution": "noop", "reason": "no in-flight real action before crash"}]
  1518	        gw = self._cold_start_gateway
  1519	        out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  1520	        for action in tick.get("actions", []):
  1521	            kind = action.get("kind")
  1522	            target = action.get("target", "")
  1523	            if kind in (ACTION_RELABEL, ACTION_TRANSITION):
  1524	                # Idempotent → safe to complete (re-issue) on recovery.
  1525	                if kind == ACTION_TRANSITION and action.get("params", {}).get(
  1526	                    "to_status"
  1527	                ) == "Under Review":
  1528	                    gw.transition_under_review(target)
  1529	                resolution = "completed"
  1530	            elif kind == ACTION_FILE_TICKET:
  1531	                # Not safely reversible/repeatable — surface to operator.
  1532	                gw.mention_operator(
  1533	                    target,
  1534	                    f"[cold-start L6] crash interrupted a file_ticket action on {target}; "
  1535	                    "verify the dependency ticket exists and was not double-filed.",
  1536	                    urgency="medium",
  1537	                )
  1538	                resolution = "escalated"
  1539	            else:
  1540	                resolution = "noop"
  1541	            out.append({"kind": kind, "target": target, "resolution": resolution})
  1542	        return out
  1543	
  1544	    def _read_recent_decision_log(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  1545	        """Return decision-log records from the last :data:`L6_REPLAY_WINDOW_HOURS`.
  1546	
  1547	        Reads the day-partitioned JSONL files spanning the window (today + the
  1548	        days it reaches back into), in chronological order, skipping malformed
  1549	        lines so a single torn record never aborts recovery.
  1550	        """
  1551	        now = self._clock()
  1552	        window_start = now - timedelta(hours=L6_REPLAY_WINDOW_HOURS)
  1553	        records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  1554	        day = window_start.date()
  1555	        seen_paths: list[Path] = []
  1675	        report = self.startup()
  1676	        if report.phase1_halted:
  1677	            logger.error(
  1678	                "[pipeline_coordinator] cold-start halted in Startup-1 "
  1679	                "(infra still down: %s) — not entering steady-state loop",
  1680	                report.still_down_units,
  1681	            )
  1682	            return 0
  1683	        return self.run_forever(install_signals=install_signals, max_ticks=max_ticks)
  1684	
  1685	    # ── entrypoints ──
  1686	
  1687	    def run_once(self) -> DecisionResult:
  1688	        """One tick: heartbeat → refresh world → evaluate → log decision.
  1689	
  1690	        Returns the engine's :class:`DecisionResult` (a no-op in the
  1691	        skeleton) so callers/tests can assert on it directly.
  1692	        """
  1693	        self._heartbeat.touch()
  1694	        self._refresh_work_graph()
  1695	        self._append_source_event_records()
  1696	        ctx = self.build_context()
  1697	        if self._config.capacity_path is not None:
  1698	            write_capacity_snapshot(ctx.capacity, self._config.capacity_path)
  1699	        result = self._engine.evaluate(ctx)
  1700	        # Hand each real (non-noop) action to the action layer. In shadow
  1701	        # mode it only records the would-be execution; the outcomes go into
  1702	        # the decision-log line so a fired rule is observable end-to-end.
  1703	        action_results = [
  1704	            self._action_executor(a, ctx)
  1705	            for a in result.actions
  1706	            if not isinstance(a, NoopAction)
  1707	        ]
  1708	        record = self._build_tick_record(result, action_results)
  1709	        self._decision_log.append(record)
  1710	        self._run_learning_loop(record, result)
  1711	        return result
  1712	
  1713	    def _run_learning_loop(self, record: dict[str, Any], result: DecisionResult) -> None:
  1714	        """Write back a Tier-2 decision + fire any due daily/weekly handler.
  1715	
  1716	        Gated on a wired learning loop (29f-11) so the skeleton / test daemon is
  1717	        unaffected. Write-back happens in-tick so a Tier-2 decision reaches
  1718	        Cognee well within the 60s Integration-AC budget; the daily + weekly
  1719	        schedules are gated by the loop's own cadence (mirrors the hourly
  1720	        sweep). Fail-open: a learning-loop error never breaks a decision tick.
  1721	        """
  1722	        loop = self._learning_loop
  1723	        if loop is None:
  1724	            return
  1725	        try:
  1845	        def _handler(signum: int, _frame: Any) -> None:
  1846	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] caught signal %d — draining", signum)
  1847	            self.request_stop()
  1848	
  1849	        for sig in (signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGINT):
  1850	            try:
  1851	                signal.signal(sig, _handler)
  1852	            except (ValueError, OSError) as exc:
  1853	                # Non-main thread / unsupported platform — caller drives
  1854	                # stop via request_stop() instead.
  1855	                logger.debug(
  1856	                    "[pipeline_coordinator] could not install handler for %s: %s",
  1857	                    sig, exc,
  1858	                )
  1859	        self._signals_installed = True
  1860	
  1861	
  1862	def build_default_coordinator(
  1863	    config: CoordinatorConfig | None = None,
  1864	    *,
  1865	    acting: bool = False,
  1866	    action_executor: ActionExecutor | None = None,
  1867	) -> PipelineCoordinator:
  1868	    """Production wiring: env config, hybrid Tier-1+Tier-2 engine, shadow layer.
  1869	
  1870	    29f-3 shipped the Tier-1 rule engine; 29f-6 fronts it with the Tier-2 LLM
  1871	    consultant (:class:`HybridDecisionEngine`) so a tick that no Tier-1 rule
  1872	    matches (or that the personality mode escalates) consults the claude CLI
  1873	    under a daily budget cap. Constructing the engine logs the loaded rule
  1874	    registry (Deploy AC). The budget guard rebuilds the day's spend from the
  1875	    decision log (ADR §3.2 stateless-across-restarts). The action layer
  1876	    defaults to shadow (observe-only) so this stays safe for the 29f-14 7-day
  1877	    shadow canary. ``acting=True`` is the single switch that permits the main
  1878	    action executor, the hourly sprint re-plan handler, AND the 4-phase
  1879	    cold-start recovery to mutate; ``acting=False`` wires every one of them
  1880	    observe-only (cold-start via :class:`ShadowColdStartGateway`, OP-1555).
  1881	    """
  1882	    config = config or CoordinatorConfig.from_env()
  1883	    if acting and action_executor is None:
  1884	        raise ValueError("acting=True requires an explicit action_executor")
  1885	    if not acting and action_executor is not None:
  1886	        raise ValueError("action_executor requires acting=True")
  1887	    decision_log = DecisionLog(config.decision_log_dir)
  1888	    # 29f-11: the learning loop reads + appends to the same decision log; build
  1889	    # it from the log so its daily/weekly schedule survives a restart (§3.2).
  1890	    from backend.agents.learning_loop import build_default_learning_loop
  1891	
  1892	    return PipelineCoordinator(
  1893	        config,
  1894	        engine=build_hybrid_engine(decision_log_dir=config.decision_log_dir),
  1895	        action_executor=action_executor,
  1896	        cold_start_gateway=_default_cold_start_gateway(config, acting=acting),
  1897	        sprint_replan_handler=build_default_sprint_replan_handler(
  1898	            config_dir=config.config_dir,
  1899	            decision_log_dir=config.decision_log_dir,
  1900	            capacity_path=config.capacity_path,
  1901	            agent_class=config.jira_agent_class,
  1902	            shadow=not acting,
  1903	        ),
  1904	        decision_log=decision_log,
  1905	        learning_loop=build_default_learning_loop(decision_log),
  1906	    )
  1907	
  1908	
  1909	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
  1910	    """CLI entrypoint. ``--once`` runs a single tick; default runs forever."""
  1911	    import argparse
  1912	
  1913	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
  1914	    parser.add_argument(
  1915	        "--once",
  1916	        action="store_true",
  1917	        help="run a single tick (heartbeat + one no-op decision) and exit "
  1918	             "(skips cold-start recovery — a diagnostic tick, not a boot)",
  1919	    )
  1920	    parser.add_argument(
  1921	        "--no-cold-start",
  1922	        action="store_true",
  1923	        help="enter the steady-state loop directly, skipping the 4-phase "
  1924	             "cold-start recovery (ADR §3.3) — for local debugging only",
  1925	    )

  1934	def transition_back_to_todo(
  1935	    client: DispatchClient,
  1936	    key: str,
  1937	    reason: str,
  1938	    idem_key: str | None = None,
  1939	    *,
  1940	    failure_class: "str | None" = None,
  1941	    raw_traceback: str = "",
  1942	    mutex_label: str | None = None,
  1943	    area: str | None = None,
  1944	) -> None:
  1945	    """In Progress → TODO with reason comment + clear assignee.
  1946	
  1947	    OP-1541 invariant: this is a destructive recovery — it clears the
  1948	    assignee. Under a shared JIRA account ONLY the current winning
  1949	    fencing-token claim holder may revert. Callers on the TOCTOU /
  1950	    assignee-divergence path MUST first prove claim ownership (see
  1951	    :func:`is_winning_claim_owner` and ``auto-runner-jira._handle_toctou_abort``)
  1952	    so a sibling instance's assignee clear does not cascade into a false
  1953	    revert (OP-1533 burst → stoploss). The non-recovery callers (CLI
  1954	    failure, capability gate, discovered dependency, pre-claim cwd-unsafe)
  1955	    are unaffected — they own or predate the claim.
  1956	
  1957	    OP-854 (C2): when ``failure_class`` is supplied, an incident row
  1958	    lands in ``runner_incidents`` before the transition fires so the
  1959	    C8 failure-graph and the C2 recall path can pick it up next time
  1960	    the ticket is attempted. Recording is best-effort — a recorder
  1961	    fault never blocks the JIRA transition (the operator still needs
  1962	    the ticket reverted even if the audit shim is wedged).
  1963	    """
  1964	    base_key = idem_key or f"transition-{key}-back-to-todo-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
  1965	    if failure_class is not None:
  1966	        try:
  1967	            # Local import keeps jira_dispatch importable without the
  1968	            # incident_recorder + failure_class chain (e.g. early-boot
  1969	            # JQL-only smoke tests).
  1970	            from backend.agents.incident_recorder import record_runner_incident
  1971	
  1972	            record_runner_incident(
  1973	                ticket_key=key,
  1974	                failure_class=failure_class,
  1975	                summary=reason.splitlines()[0] if reason else "",
  1976	                raw_traceback=raw_traceback,
  1977	                runner_class=getattr(client, "agent_class", "unknown"),
  1978	                mutex_label=mutex_label,
  1979	                area=area,
  1980	            )
  1981	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — best-effort wiring
  1982	            log.warning(
  1983	                "jira_dispatch.record_runner_incident_failed key=%s err=%s",
  1984	                key,
  1985	                exc,
  1986	            )
  1987	    add_comment(
  1988	        client, key, f"Reverting to TODO. Reason:\n{reason}",
  1989	        idem_key=f"{base_key}-comment",
  1990	    )
  1991	    clear_assignee(client, key, idem_key=f"{base_key}-clear-assignee")
  1992	    _request_idempotent(
  1993	        client, "POST", f"/issue/{key}/transitions",
  1994	        {"transition": {"id": TRANSITION_IDS["back_to_todo"]}},
  1995	        f"{base_key}-transition",
  1996	    )
  1997	
  1998	    # OP-1140: stoploss circuit-breaker recording — best-effort.
  1999	    # A recorder fault (network blip, label-fetch 404 on a freshly-created
  2000	    # ticket, ...) must NEVER prevent the §11-revert from completing; the
  2001	    # operator still needs the ticket re-armed for triage even if the
  2002	    # counter slips one tick.
  2003	    try:
  2004	        from backend.agents import runner_stoploss
  2005	
  2006	        labels_now = fetch_labels(client, key)
  2007	        runner_stoploss.register_revert(client, key, labels_now)
  2008	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — best-effort wiring
  2009	        log.warning(
  2010	            "jira_dispatch.register_revert_failed key=%s err=%s", key, exc,
  2011	        )

     1	"""SQLite-backed idempotency keys for runner external mutations.
     2	
     3	The runner retries whole ticks.  Without a local dedup store, retried
     4	JIRA comments and transitions can create duplicate operator-visible
     5	side effects.  This module keeps successful mutation responses for 24h
     6	under ``~/.config/omnisight/idem-keys.db`` and returns the cached
     7	response when the same key is seen again.
     8	"""
     9	
    10	from __future__ import annotations
    11	
    12	import functools
    13	import json
    14	import os
    15	import sqlite3
    16	import time
    17	from pathlib import Path
    18	from typing import Any, Callable, TypeVar
    19	
    20	
    21	def _resolve_idempotency_db_path() -> Path:
    22	    """Resolve the SQLite path from env override or fall back to the legacy default.
    23	
    24	    OP-783: a multi-instance runner host runs several runner processes in
    25	    parallel; each one needs its own idempotency DB so that two
    26	    in-flight transitions for the same JIRA key (one per instance, both
    27	    targeting different tickets) don't share a row and silently dedup
    28	    each other. The launcher script sets ``OMNISIGHT_IDEMPOTENCY_DB_PATH``
    29	    to ``~/.config/omnisight/idem-keys-<bot>.db``; default-instance
    30	    runners and library callers without the env var keep the legacy
    31	    ``~/.config/omnisight/idem-keys.db`` path (backwards-compat).
    32	    """
    33	    override = os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_IDEMPOTENCY_DB_PATH", "").strip()
    34	    if override:
    35	        return Path(override).expanduser()
    36	    return Path("~/.config/omnisight/idem-keys.db").expanduser()
    37	
    38	
    39	IDEMPOTENCY_DB = _resolve_idempotency_db_path()
    40	TTL_SECONDS = 24 * 60 * 60
    41	
    42	F = TypeVar("F", bound=Callable[..., Any])
    43	
    44	
    45	class IdempotencyStore:
    46	    """Tiny SQLite cache keyed by caller-provided idempotency strings."""
    47	
    48	    def __init__(self, path: Path = IDEMPOTENCY_DB, ttl_seconds: int = TTL_SECONDS) -> None:
    49	        self.path = path
    50	        self.ttl_seconds = ttl_seconds
    51	
    52	    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
    53	        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    54	        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.path)
    55	        conn.execute(
    56	            "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS idempotency_keys ("
    57	            "key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, response TEXT NOT NULL, ts REAL NOT NULL)"
    58	        )
    59	        return conn
    60	
    61	    def prune(self, now: float | None = None) -> None:
    62	        cutoff = (now if now is not None else time.time()) - self.ttl_seconds
    63	        with self._connect() as conn:
    64	            conn.execute("DELETE FROM idempotency_keys WHERE ts < ?", (cutoff,))
    65	
    66	    def get(self, key: str, now: float | None = None) -> Any | None:
    67	        current = now if now is not None else time.time()
    68	        with self._connect() as conn:
    69	            row = conn.execute(
    70	                "SELECT response, ts FROM idempotency_keys WHERE key = ?",
    71	                (key,),
    72	            ).fetchone()
    73	            if row is None:
    74	                return None
    75	            response, ts = row
    76	            if ts < current - self.ttl_seconds:
    77	                conn.execute("DELETE FROM idempotency_keys WHERE key = ?", (key,))
    78	                return None
    79	            return json.loads(response)
    80	
    81	    def put(self, key: str, response: Any, now: float | None = None) -> None:
    82	        current = now if now is not None else time.time()
    83	        with self._connect() as conn:
    84	            conn.execute(
    85	                "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO idempotency_keys (key, response, ts) "
    86	                "VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
    87	                (key, json.dumps(response, sort_keys=True, default=str), current),
    88	            )
    89	
    90	    def run(self, key: str, fn: Callable[[], Any]) -> Any:
    91	        self.prune()
    92	        cached = self.get(key)
    93	        if cached is not None:
    94	            return cached
    95	        response = fn()
    96	        self.put(key, response if response is not None else {})
    97	        return response
    98	
    99	
   100	DEFAULT_STORE = IdempotencyStore()
   101	
   102	
   103	def idempotent(key_arg: str = "idem_key", store: IdempotencyStore = DEFAULT_STORE) -> Callable[[F], F]:
   104	    """Decorator for functions that accept an ``idem_key`` keyword."""
   105	
   106	    def decorate(fn: F) -> F:
   107	        @functools.wraps(fn)
   108	        def wrapped(*args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
   109	            key = kwargs.get(key_arg)
   110	            if not key:
   111	                return fn(*args, **kwargs)
   112	            return store.run(str(key), lambda: fn(*args, **kwargs))
   113	
   114	        return wrapped  # type: ignore[return-value]
   115	
   116	    return decorate

    Used so a record's own timestamp picks the day-file (rather than the

# ── Action layer (ADR-0021 §6) — shadow stub for 29f-3 ────────────────


class ShadowActionExecutor:
    """Records actions instead of executing them (ADR §6 / shadow mode).

    The real §6 action layer (JIRA mutate / @-operator) lands in a later
    phase; until then — and during the 29f-14 7-day shadow canary — the
    coordinator must *observe* what a rule would do without touching JIRA.
    This executor returns a per-action result record (``executed=False``)
    that the daemon folds into the decision-log line, so the integration AC
    ("rule returns Action → action layer executes it (mock if not yet built)
    → action recorded in decision log") is satisfied without any mutation.

    A real executor is a drop-in: same ``execute`` signature, returning
    ``executed=True`` plus the mutation outcome.
    """

    #: Stamped into each result so a decision-log reader can tell shadow
    #: (observed-only) executions from real ones.
    executed: bool = False

    def execute(self, action: Action, ctx: DecisionContext) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "kind": action.kind,
            "target": action.target,
            "executed": self.executed,
            "shadow": True,
        }


# Action executor seam: anything with ``execute(action, ctx) -> dict``.
ActionExecutor = Callable[[Action, DecisionContext], dict]


# ── Event sources (ADR-0021 §4) ──────────────────────────────────────


class EventDeduper:
    """Five-minute coalescing window for identical source events."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        window_seconds: float = DEFAULT_EVENT_DEDUPE_SECONDS,
        clock: Clock = _utc_now,
    ) -> None:
        self._window = timedelta(seconds=window_seconds)
        self._clock = clock
        self._seen: dict[str, datetime] = {}

    def fresh(self, event: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        key = self._key(event)
        now = self._clock()
        last = self._seen.get(key)
        if last is not None and now - last < self._window:
            return False
        self._seen[key] = now
        return True

    def _key(self, event: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        payload = event.get("payload")
        if isinstance(payload, dict):
            change = payload.get("change")
            change = change if isinstance(change, dict) else {}
            parts = [
                str(event.get("source") or ""),
                str(payload.get("type") or payload.get("webhookEvent") or ""),
                str(payload.get("id") or ""),
                str(change.get("id") or ""),
                str(change.get("number") or ""),
                str(payload.get("key") or ""),
            ]
            if any(parts[1:]):
                return "\x1f".join(parts)
        return json.dumps(event, sort_keys=True, default=str)


class BridgeEventTailer:
    """Tail the bridge JSONL event tap without blocking the coordinator."""

    def __init__(self, path: Path) -> None:
        self._path = Path(path)
        self._offset = 0

    @property
    def path(self) -> Path:
        return self._path

    def poll(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        if not self._path.exists():
            return []
        size = self._path.stat().st_size
        if size < self._offset:
            self._offset = 0
        events: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        with self._path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            fh.seek(self._offset)
            for line in fh:
                if not line.strip():
                    continue
                try:
                    record = json.loads(line)
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    continue
                payload = record.get("event") if isinstance(record, dict) else record
                if isinstance(payload, dict):
                    events.append(
                        {
                            "source": "bridge",
                            "trigger": f"bridge-event:{payload.get('type', 'unknown')}",
                            "payload": payload,
                            "bridge_record_ts": record.get("ts")
                            if isinstance(record, dict)
                            else None,
                        }
                    )
            self._offset = fh.tell()
        return events


class JiraEventPoller:
    """60s JIRA poll for coordinator-triggering ticket states."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        agent_class: str,
        clock: Clock = _utc_now,
        interval_seconds: float = DEFAULT_JIRA_POLL_INTERVAL_SECONDS,
        client_factory: Callable[[str], Any] | None = None,
        search: Callable[[Any, str], list[dict[str, Any]]] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._agent_class = agent_class
        self._clock = clock
        self._interval = timedelta(seconds=interval_seconds)
        self._client_factory = client_factory
        self._search = search
        self._client: Any | None = None
        self._last_poll: datetime | None = None

    def due(self) -> bool:
        return (
            self._last_poll is None
            or self._clock() - self._last_poll >= self._interval
        )

    def poll(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        if not self.due():
            return []
        self._last_poll = self._clock()
        try:
            client = self._client or self._make_client()
            self._client = client
            issues = self._search_issues(client, coordinator_jql(client.project_key))
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — source failure is non-fatal
            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] jira poll failed: %s", exc)
            return []
        events: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for issue in issues:
            key = str(issue.get("key") or "")
            if not key:
                continue
            events.append(
                {
                    "source": "jira",
                    "trigger": "jira-poll",
                    "ticket_key": key,
                    "payload": issue,
                }
            )
        return events

    def _make_client(self) -> Any:
        if self._client_factory is not None:
            return self._client_factory(self._agent_class)
        from backend.agents import jira_dispatch

        return jira_dispatch.make_client(self._agent_class)

    def _search_issues(self, client: Any, jql: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        if self._search is not None:
            return self._search(client, jql)
        from backend.agents import jira_dispatch

        resp = jira_dispatch._request(
            client,
            "POST",
            "/search/jql",
            {
                "jql": jql,
                "fields": ["summary", "labels", "status", "assignee", "updated"],
                "maxResults": 100,
            },
        )
        return list(resp.get("issues", []))


def coordinator_jql(project_key: str) -> str:
    """ADR-0021 §4 JQL for the 60s coordinator poll."""

    return (
        f'project = "{project_key}" '
        'AND (labels is EMPTY OR labels not in ("coord-skip")) '
        "AND ("
        'labels = "needs-coordinator" '
        'OR (status in ("Done", "Closed", "Won\'t Do", "却下") '
        "AND statusCategoryChangedDate >= -1d) "
        'OR (status = "進行中" AND assignee is EMPTY)'
        ") "
        "ORDER BY updated ASC"
    )

# Cold-start 4-phase recovery (29f-7 / OP-1005) — ADR-0021 §3.3 + §9 L6
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#
# "The coordinator is *the* canonical startup orchestrator — if it is healthy,
# everything else is" (ADR §2.4). On boot it runs four phases before entering
# the steady-state loop:
#
#   Startup-1  infra verify   — deployment-audit.sh + systemctl --user start
#   (L6)       crash recovery — replay last 24h decision log (ADR §9 L6)

@dataclass
class ColdStartReport:
    """Mutable accumulator for what the 4-phase recovery did (one boot)."""

    started_units: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    still_down_units: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    phase1_halted: bool = False
    crash_recoveries: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    reconciled: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    swept: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    entered_loop: bool = False


@runtime_checkable
class ColdStartGateway(Protocol):
    """The infra / JIRA / git seam the 4-phase recovery drives (ADR §3.3).

    Every method is best-effort in production: an adapter that cannot reach
    its backend returns an empty / conservative answer rather than raising,
    so a degraded environment can never wedge the daemon before its loop.
    """

    # ── Startup-1 (infra) ──
    def audit_infra(self) -> InfraAuditResult: ...
    def start_unit(self, unit: str) -> bool: ...

    # ── Startup-2 (JIRA reconcile) ──
    def interrupted_tickets(self) -> list[InterruptedTicket]: ...
    def has_live_runner(self, key: str) -> bool: ...
    def gerrit_change_mergeable(self, key: str) -> bool: ...
    def branch_has_commits(self, key: str) -> bool: ...
    def mark_resumable(self, key: str) -> None: ...
    def transition_under_review(self, key: str) -> None: ...
    def reset_to_todo(self, key: str) -> None: ...

    # ── Startup-3 (stale sweep) ──
    def stale_sweep_plan(self) -> StaleSweepPlan: ...
    def remove_label(self, key: str, label: str) -> None: ...
    def clear_assignee(self, key: str) -> None: ...

    38	---
    39	
    40	## §dry_run — the one OPEN DECISION for codex round-3 (Q7)
    41	
    42	Confirmed: no engine path emits `dry_run=False` today (rules.py:126 default,
    43	Tier-1 pass-through :904, Tier-2 pass-through llm:1073/1132). So a literal
    44	"only mutate when `dry_run=False`" executor would observe-only forever. v3 must
    45	choose where the flip happens. **Two options, trade-offs stated; codex round-3
    46	picks one** (this is the architecture fork):
    47	
    48	### Option A — executor ignores `dry_run` in acting mode
    49	`LiveActionExecutor` is constructed only on the `acting=True` path
    50	(build_default_coordinator:1883), so its mere existence means "mutate". It
    51	ignores the per-action `dry_run` flag entirely; the flag stays meaningful only
    52	for `ShadowActionExecutor`.
    53	- **+** smallest diff (no engine/factory change); single source of truth = the
    54	  env-gate + which executor is wired.
    55	- **+** no risk of a half-flipped state (some actions real, some not).
    56	- **−** the per-action `dry_run` field becomes dead in acting mode; a future
    57	  per-action dry-run (e.g. "shadow just this risky kind") needs new plumbing.
    58	- **−** decision-log `dry_run:true` lines (logged from the action, :1358) would
    59	  be misleading in acting mode unless the executor overwrites the logged flag.
    60	
    61	### Option B — engine seam rewrites actions to `dry_run=False` when acting
    62	A thin wrapper between `engine.evaluate` and the executor (run_once:1699-1703)
    63	rebuilds each `Action` with `dry_run=False` when `acting=True`.
    64	- **+** `dry_run` stays a real per-action property end-to-end; decision-log is
    65	  accurate; enables future per-kind shadow-in-acting.
    66	- **+** executor logic is uniform (always "mutate iff `dry_run=False`").
    67	- **−** larger diff; `Action` is a frozen dataclass (rules.py:80) → rebuild via
    68	  `dataclasses.replace`, touching every action each tick.
    69	- **−** a second place (the wrapper) must stay in sync with the env-gate.
    70	
    71	**v3 recommendation: Option A** (smallest blast radius for a first live flip;
    72	the per-action dry-run nicety is YAGNI today). But this is the fork we want
    73	codex to rule on with fresh eyes.
    74	
    75	---
    76	
    77	## Idempotency (B3 / Q8) — corrected grain
    78	
    79	- Key = `coord:{kind}:{target}:{content_hash}` where `content_hash` is a stable
    80	  digest of the action's semantic payload (`params`), **independent of
    81	  `decision_id`** — so the same logical action on two ticks dedupes (the bug
    82	  round-2 caught: decision_id is fresh per tick, rules.py:266).
    83	- Composite actions issue multiple REST calls; `_request_idempotent` caches by
    84	  key (jira_dispatch.py:331), so each call gets a distinct **suffix**:
    85	  `:add:<label>`, `:remove:<label>`, `:clear-assignee`, `:transition`,
    86	  `:comment`. (Otherwise a relabel adding 2 labels would dedupe the 2nd PUT.)
    87	- The named low-level helpers already accept `idem_key`
    88	  (`transition_to_under_review_if_needed` :1649, `transition_back_to_todo`
    89	  :1934, comment/label/assignee :2014) — **no signature change needed**, only
    90	  key discipline.
    91	
    92	---
    93	
    94	## §LiveActionExecutor dispatch (carried from v2, unchanged vocabulary)
    95	
    96	Dispatch on `action.kind` (sprint_replan.py:141 pattern) → low-level
    97	`jira_dispatch` via a client built from `config.jira_agent_class` (fixes S9):
    98	
    99	| kind | live op | idem suffix |
   100	|---|---|---|
   101	| `transition` | `transition_back_to_todo` / `transition_to_under_review_if_needed` per `to_status` + **destructive-recheck for To Do** | `:transition` |
   102	| `relabel` | `add_label`/`remove_label`; ride-along `clear_assignee` on claim-label removal | `:add:<l>`/`:remove:<l>`/`:clear-assignee` |
   103	| `mention_operator` | `add_comment(@op)`; label iff `urgency=="high"` (:807) | `:comment`(+`:label`) |
   104	| `escalate` | `add_label("needs-operator-action")` **+** `add_comment(reason)` — always both (B2) | `:label`+`:comment` |
   105	| `mark_for_followup` | `add_label("coord-resume-after:{when}")` + comment | `:label`+`:comment` |
   106	| `file_ticket` | NOT auto-executed → `mention_operator` + 2 result lines (accounting fix) | `:comment`+`:label` |
   107	| `noop` / unknown | none → `executed:False` (`reason:"unsupported"` for unknown) | — |
   108	
   109	Outcome: real `executed:True/False` because we call low-level helpers (which
   110	raise on failure) inside a per-action `try/except`, NOT the fail-open gateway
   111	that swallows exceptions (:746/:762/:810).
   112	
   113	---
   114	
   115	## §Blast-radius (B4) — cap + fairness
   116	
   117	`run_once` (:1699-1707) gains:
   118	1. `OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_MAX_ACTIONS_PER_TICK` (default **1**). Excess →
   119	   `executed:False,reason:"tick_cap"`, logged.
   120	2. **Fairness cursor**: a persisted round-robin over targets so consecutive
   121	   capped ticks drain a backlog rather than re-picking the same focal ticket
   122	   (addresses round-2's starvation concern for one-action-per-ticket sweeps).
   123	3. Per-action `try/except` so one failure logs + the tick still records (S7).
   124	4. Kill-switch `OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_ACTING_KILL=1` → observe-only without
   125	   restart.
   126	
   127	---
   128	
   129	## §Cold-start budget (B6) — per-phase accounting
   130	
   131	Distinct caps, each defining "mutation" for its phase:
   132	- Startup-1: `start_unit` systemctl starts (:653) — cap N₁.
   133	- Startup-2: JIRA transitions/labels/comments (:735) — cap N₂.
   134	- Startup-3: stale-sweep relabel/clear loop (:776) — cap N₃.
   135	Env: `OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_COLD_START_MAX_{INFRA,RECONCILE,SWEEP}`. First flip
   136	in an env with a known-small interrupted set; `cold_start_complete=[]` is NOT a
   137	safety property (round-1 B6).
   138	
   139	---
   140	
   141	## §Crash recovery (S10 / Q11) — intent-log + persisted outcomes
   142	
   143	1. **Intent-before-execute**: `run_once` writes a `tick_intent` record (planned
   144	   actions + their idem keys) BEFORE executing (closes the :1703-before-:1708
   145	   window). After execution it writes the outcome record.
   146	2. **Persist `action_results`** alongside `actions` in the tick record (:1394)
   147	   so L6 reads per-subcall outcomes.
   148	3. L6 (`_resolve_interrupted_actions` :1514) replays any `tick_intent` whose
   149	   outcome record is missing, re-issuing under the SAME stable idem key →
   150	   idempotent, no double-apply. Recovery stays keyed on the ADR `ACTION_*`
   151	   vocabulary (no gateway-method names introduced).
   152	
   153	---
   154	
   155	## §coord-skip / quarantine recheck (N13) — fail-closed
   156	Before any mutation the executor re-reads live labels of `action.target`
   157	(`jira_dispatch.get_issue_labels` or equivalent) and:
   158	- `coord-skip`/`coord-quarantine` present → `executed:False,reason:"coord_skip"`.
   159	- label read FAILS → `executed:False,reason:"label_read_failed"` (fail-CLOSED;
   160	  never mutate on unknown state). Closes the Tier-2 gap (rules.py:611 guards
   161	  only Tier-1).
   162	
   163	---
   164	
   165	## §Activation (rewritten — repo reality)
   166	The deployed unit sets env **inline** via `Environment=`
   167	(pipeline-coordinator.service:29-32); there is **no EnvironmentFile**
   168	(coordinator-runbook.md:53). So activation is a **systemd drop-in**, not an
   169	`env.conf`:
   170	1. Phased: first flip in an env with a small interrupted set; verify per-phase
   171	   cold-start caps not exceeded.
   172	2. `systemctl --user edit pipeline-coordinator` → add
   173	   `Environment=OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_ACTING=1` and remove the
   174	   `OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_DAILY_BUDGET_USD=0` override (both together — D5).
   175	   Confirm `MAX_ACTIONS_PER_TICK=1`.
   176	3. `systemctl --user daemon-reload && systemctl --user restart pipeline-coordinator`.
   177	4. Watch decision-log for `executed:True`; verify intent matches. Rollback =
   178	   `OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_ACTING_KILL=1` (no restart) OR revert the drop-in +
   179	   restart.
   180	
   181	**Authorization scope**: this is a daemon env authorization. The coordinator
   182	daemon is NOT capability-matrix gated (capability_matrix.yaml:27 `jira_update`
   183	governs runner pickup, a different actor).
   184	
   185	---
   186	
   187	## §Tests (v3)
   188	Carried from v2 plus:
   189	- Idempotency: same logical action on two ticks (different decision_id) → ONE
   190	  effective mutation (the Q8 regression test).
   191	- Composite suffix: relabel adding 2 labels → 2 distinct PUTs, neither deduped.
   192	- Destructive recheck: `transition→To Do` with a now-live runner → abstains.
   193	- Crash window: intent written before execute; missing-outcome intent replays
   194	  idempotently.
   195	- coord-skip fail-closed: label read raises → no mutation.
   196	- **Update existing**: `test_l6_resolves_interrupted_actions_by_kind`
   197	  (coldstart:473), `test_default_cold_start_gateway_shadow_vs_acting`
   198	  (coldstart:954, assert agent_class), `test_pipeline_coordinator_systemd.py:68`
   199	  (new env assertions).
   200	
   201	---
   202	
   203	## Open questions for codex (round-3)
   204	- **Q7 (the fork)**: pick Option A (executor ignores dry_run in acting) vs
   205	  Option B (engine seam rewrites to dry_run=False). v3 recommends A.
   206	- Q12: is `content_hash` over `params` enough for the idem grain, or must it
   207	  include `kind`+`target` only (params may carry volatile fields)?
   208	- Q13: fairness cursor persistence — reuse the decision-log, or a separate
   209	  small state file? (Daemon is stateless-across-restarts by design, ADR §3.2.)
   210	- Q14: per-phase cold-start caps — safe default integers (N₁/N₂/N₃)?
   211	- Q15: anything v3 STILL misses.
   212	
   213	## Audit asks for codex (round-3)
   214	1. Re-confirm B3/B4/B6/S10/N12/N13 + the 4 new BLOCKERs (destructive transition,
   215	   env reality, crash window Q11, dispatch) are RESOLVED, with file:line.
   216	2. Rule on the Q7 fork (A vs B) with reasoning.
   217	3. Final verdict: file-ready / file-ready-with-conditions / needs-v4.

- Q11: v2 still misses crash windows before decision-log append. `run_once` executes actions before building/appending the tick record [pipeline_coordinator.py:1703](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1703), [pipeline_coordinator.py:1708](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1708). A process death after mutation but before append is invisible to L6.

**SHOULD-FIX**

- Q10: 1/tick is safe for first live flip but can starve legitimate sweeps. The current poll may surface many tickets, and rules like stale-claim-cleanup produce one relabel per ticket [pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:707](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:707). Keep default 1, but design needs queue/fairness metrics and a documented ramp, not just an env knob.

- JIRA helper idem-key threading is not a prerequisite signature change for the named helpers; they already accept `idem_key`. The prerequisite is composite-key discipline: relabel with multiple labels must not reuse one idem key for multiple PUTs because `_request_idempotent` caches by key [jira_dispatch.py:331](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/jira_dispatch.py:331). Use suffixes like `:add:<label>`, `:remove:<label>`, `:clear-assignee`.

- Capability/systemd gap: `jira_update` exists in the runner capability vocabulary [capability_matrix.yaml:27](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/config/capability_matrix.yaml:27), but the coordinator daemon itself is not capability-matrix gated. v2 should say this is a daemon env authorization, not runner pickup capability.

**NICE**

- `file_ticket` “not auto-executed + mention_operator” consumes one cap slot but performs a comment/label side effect. v2 should define whether this counts as executing `file_ticket`, `mention_operator`, or both in `action_results`, otherwise decision-log readers will misinterpret `executed:False` with side effects.

Verdict: needs-v3.

3238:docs/adr/ADR-0042-image-distribution-strategy-ghcr-decommission.md:15:The GHCR→GitLab CR migration is ~90% done (prod backend + frontend pull from the private GitLab CR `sora.services:49160`). 3239:docs/adr/ADR-0042-image-distribution-strategy-ghcr-decommission.md:21:2. **GHCR is decommissioned.** Its only unique capability — public/anonymous distribution — is not used by the SaaS model. The remaining pieces migrate or retire (all CR-ready, none has a fundamental mismatch):
3252:docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:22:2. §2 — the contract surface: probe + gauge + flag + capability + UI.
3253:docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:31:This spec is the **contract** — not the implementation. Code lands in `v2-⑨-1bc` (the `ai_core_probe` reference implementation + Prometheus gauge + in-process flag), `v2-⑨-2bc` (the `llm_fallback_chain` builder consumer + capability-inventory propagation), and `v2-⑨-Integration` (chaos test); operator-side hygiene for the current `ai_gateway` container lands in `v2-⑨-3a-Decision`. Section anchors in §2, §3, §4, §5 are stable and will be referenced from those tickets' AC.
3254:docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:45:| Available | `omnisight_aux_service_available{service="…"} == 1` | Service is **exposed**: included in capability inventory; included in fallback chains where applicable; UI shows the corresponding feature enabled; capability-aware code paths may delegate to it. | Capability inventory carries `capability:enable=<service>`; UI tile lit; Prom gauge `== 1`. |
3255:docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:46:| Unavailable | `omnisight_aux_service_available{service="…"} == 0` | Service is **quietly skipped**: removed from capability inventory; removed from fallback chains; UI hides the feature (no error toast, no degraded badge — the feature is simply not visible to the end-user); capability-aware code paths route around it. | Capability inventory does NOT carry `capability:enable=<service>`; UI tile not rendered; Prom gauge `== 0`; after 24 h continuous unavailability, a `severity=warn` alert fires (§4). |
3256:docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:60:- **NOT** a request-time fallback. The flag is consulted by *builders* (the `llm_fallback_chain` builder, the capability-inventory builder) — not by per-request hot paths. Per-request code reads the *already-built* chain. This keeps the hot path free of probe-state reads.
3257:docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:64:The 2026-05-14 reframe (operator Q3) deliberately raised the abstraction layer from "ai-core hygiene" to "optional auxiliary service contract" precisely because the same pattern will be needed for omnisight-ai-engine (2026-Q3 plan) and omnisight-vision-core (2026-Q4 plan). The cost of writing the spec service-agnostic is near-zero (substitute `<service>` for `ai_core` in §2 and §3); the cost of *not* doing so is that each of the future sister services re-derives the pattern, drifts in details, and the cross-family invariants (alert severity, gauge name, capability propagation) silently disagree. §5 covers extensibility; §2 names "the auxiliary service" rather than "ai-core" throughout.
3258:docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:105:- The gauge MUST NOT be exposed for services that are not registered (calling `.labels(service="…")` with an unregistered name is a programmer error and is rejected by §2.4 capability-inventory schema validation at builder time, not at probe time).
3259:docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:126:The capability inventory builder MUST consult `AUX_SERVICE_AVAILABLE` when deciding which `capability:enable=<service>` labels to expose. Specifically:
3261:docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:129:- When `AUX_SERVICE_AVAILABLE[<name>] is True`, the inventory carries `capability:enable=<service>`.
3262:docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:130:- When `AUX_SERVICE_AVAILABLE[<name>] is False`, the inventory **does not carry** that label — neither `capability:enable=<service>=false` nor `capability:disable=<service>`. The contract is *absence* not *negation*. This matches the existing `capability:enable=*` label convention from `docs/sop/jira-label-conventions.md` §"capability inventory".
3263:docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:131:- Downstream consumers of the capability inventory (the JIRA dispatcher's `capability:enable=*` matching for runner pickup, the UI's feature-tile renderer, the per-request RBAC layer's deferred-feature check) MUST treat absence as "feature unavailable" — no further check needed.
3264:docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:146:The frontend reads the capability inventory (§2.4); rendering decisions cascade from there. No frontend code needs to know about probes, gauges, or flags directly — that boundary stays inside `backend/`.
3265:docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:286:            seeing errors; capability-aware features are silently absent.
3266:docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:327:- Alternative: the install's compose / deploy config sets `OMNISIGHT_AUX_SERVICE_DISABLE=ai_core,vision_core` (comma-separated). When a service is in this list, the probe is **not started** and no samples reach the gauge, so the 24 h-avg condition is never evaluated. The capability inventory simply never adds the corresponding `capability:enable=<service>` label, and the UI does not render the feature. This is the "we don't ship this auxiliary on this install" path.
3267:docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:352:The same probe loop, the same gauge name (different label), the same in-process flag dict (different key), the same chain-builder-style consumer (specific to whatever the new service feeds — vision-core would feed a *vision capability chain*, not the LLM chain).
3268:docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:361:4. **Wire** the corresponding capability-inventory builder branch (e.g. `if VISION_CORE_AVAILABLE: caps.append("capability:enable=vision_core")`).
3269:docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:371:- **Cross-auxiliary dependencies.** If auxiliary A depends on auxiliary B (e.g. ai-engine needs ai-core), each probe is independent. The capability-inventory builder may encode "feature X requires both ai-core AND ai-engine" but the probe contract does not.

3272:docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:502:2. **Q9.1.2 — Does the per-service convenience alias (`AI_CORE_AVAILABLE`) leak into router code, or stay inside the consumer module?** *Recommended at `v2-⑨-2bc`:* stay inside the consumer module. 3995:docs/sop/lessons/L-OP-843-vendor-claim-disambiguate-before-deleting-resilience.md:48:1. **Vendor talks compress capability layers**. Any pitch of the form "we now handle X so you don't have to" — including "no more retry boilerplate", "memory just works", "agents finish on their own" — is almost always conflating *at least* three distinct layers: transport (auto-retry HTTP), framework (agent loop / tool dispatch), and reliability (classification, breakers, resume, escalation, budget caps). The vendor genuinely ships some of these (usually the first two). The reliability layer almost always stays caller-managed because it is intrinsically deployment-specific. Don't delete reliability code on the strength of a single talk's framing.

  1831	    ``fetch_pickable_tickets`` → :func:`to_snapshot`, or a single

**BLOCKER**

- N12/Q7 — PARTIAL. v3 correctly proves no current path emits `dry_run=False`: factories default true in [pipeline_coordinator_rules.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:100), Tier-1 passes actions through in [pipeline_coordinator_rules.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:904), and Tier-2 passes through in [pipeline_coordinator_llm_consultation.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_llm_consultation.py:1073). But v3 leaves the fork open while claiming it “makes the flip explicit” in [design-v3.md](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/docs/operations/2026-05-22-coordinator-acting-live-action-executor-design-v3.md:29). Needs a chosen implementation, not an open decision.
- Q7 ruling: choose **Option B**, not v3’s Option A. `Action` is frozen, but `dataclasses.replace(action, dry_run=False)` is the right seam because `run_once` logs `actions` and top-level `dry_run` from the action objects in [pipeline_coordinator.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1358). Option A would mutate while the decision log still says `dry_run:true`, which is bad audit data and bad crash-recovery input. The extra wrapper is a small cost for truthful intent/outcome logs.
- B4 — PARTIAL. v3 adds a fairness cursor in [design-v3.md](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/docs/operations/2026-05-22-coordinator-acting-live-action-executor-design-v3.md:120), fixing starvation in principle. But Q13 admits persistence is undecided in [design-v3.md](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/docs/operations/2026-05-22-coordinator-acting-live-action-executor-design-v3.md:208), while ADR §3.2 only blesses decision-log/state artifacts in [ADR-0021](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/docs/adr/ADR-0021-release-pipeline-coordinator.md:105). Use decision-log-derived cursor state, not a new opaque file, unless ADR is amended.
- S10/Q11 crash recovery — PARTIAL. Intent-before-execute in [design-v3.md](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/docs/operations/2026-05-22-coordinator-acting-live-action-executor-design-v3.md:143) closes the current execute-before-log window in [pipeline_coordinator.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1703). But v3 still must define outcome correlation: how a `tick_intent` is matched to its outcome record, and how completed subcalls in `action_results` avoid replay. Current L6 only scans `decision_tick` plus shutdown records in [pipeline_coordinator.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1493).

**SHOULD-FIX**

- B3 — RESOLVED with one condition. Stable `coord:{kind}:{target}:{content_hash}` fixes the exact-key dedupe issue because `decision_id` is fresh per tick in [pipeline_coordinator_rules.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:266) and idempotency only dedupes exact keys in [idempotency.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/idempotency.py:90). - B6 — PARTIAL. No new helper is prerequisite, but v3 should name `fetch_ticket_labels()` and fail closed on exceptions.

- N12/Q7 — PARTIAL. v3 correctly proves no current path emits `dry_run=False`: factories default true in [pipeline_coordinator_rules.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:100), Tier-1 passes actions through in [pipeline_coordinator_rules.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:904), and Tier-2 passes through in [pipeline_coordinator_llm_consultation.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_llm_consultation.py:1073). But v3 leaves the fork open while claiming it “makes the flip explicit” in [design-v3.md](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/docs/operations/2026-05-22-coordinator-acting-live-action-executor-design-v3.md:29). Needs a chosen implementation, not an open decision.
- Q7 ruling: choose **Option B**, not v3’s Option A. `Action` is frozen, but `dataclasses.replace(action, dry_run=False)` is the right seam because `run_once` logs `actions` and top-level `dry_run` from the action objects in [pipeline_coordinator.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1358). Option A would mutate while the decision log still says `dry_run:true`, which is bad audit data and bad crash-recovery input. The extra wrapper is a small cost for truthful intent/outcome logs.
- B4 — PARTIAL. v3 adds a fairness cursor in [design-v3.md](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/docs/operations/2026-05-22-coordinator-acting-live-action-executor-design-v3.md:120), fixing starvation in principle. But Q13 admits persistence is undecided in [design-v3.md](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/docs/operations/2026-05-22-coordinator-acting-live-action-executor-design-v3.md:208), while ADR §3.2 only blesses decision-log/state artifacts in [ADR-0021](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/docs/adr/ADR-0021-release-pipeline-coordinator.md:105). Use decision-log-derived cursor state, not a new opaque file, unless ADR is amended.
